[Dremsyl's Tower, Servant's Quarters]
A thin layer of slippery skin has grown across the floor from the walls. Overhead, long, thin tentacles of the tower monster have spread across the rafters, encircling abandoned straw nests. The nests are small and tucked into the crevices in the massive wooden beams along the ceiling, no doubt once the homes of the Zacotl that served the master of the tower, and oversaw the Goblins. A thick layer of rope-like tentacles dangle from the ceiling, blocking the view to the south, as if they seek to keep something hidden.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Karishta]
A massive tiled mosaic of a ten-head hydra in the throes of casting some grand, unimaginable spell covers the entire far wall and ceiling of the room. Some tiles have fallen from the image and shattered upon the stony floor, but much of the fantastic masterpiece has survived through the ages. A cracked amethyst altar sits in the center of the room, the worn image of a black rook is carved into the surface of the immense piece of semi-precious stone. Surprisingly, the tower's flesh creature has left much of this room free of its corrupting touch, rather only a few broad tube-like tentacles have attached themselves to the ceiling and have twined around the amethyst altar and disappeared into the base of the deep purple stone.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Devin the Silvarin,  Trion the Fae
In this room are: worn rook-engraved amethyst altar
Obvious exits are: west, north

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Cassien]
Slender tentacles have crept into the temple of Cassien, unafraid of the tyrant god's wrath, and twined around pillars that were once beautiful. Now most of the obsidian and ruby inlaid columns have fallen to the ground, shattered by the crushing strength of the muscled appendages of the tower beast. A faded mural of a stalking panther covers the back wall, the intricate detail in the painting, suggests it was created by a true master of the craft in loving devotion of his vain god. An obsidian altar sits before the masterpiece, an exquisite carving of a goshawk, its wings outstretched once sat upon the volcanic stone, now it has fallen to the floor, one wing snapped off. No doubt the god Cassien has long since abandoned this place, but it still reeks of his power, and the tentacles of the fleshy monster feast off the residual magical energy of the shrine.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Devin the Silvarin,  Trion the Fae
In this room are: superbly carved obsidian altar

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Karishta]
A massive tiled mosaic of a ten-head hydra in the throes of casting some grand, unimaginable spell covers the entire far wall and ceiling of the room. [Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Cassien]
Slender tentacles have crept into the temple of Cassien, unafraid of the tyrant god's wrath, and twined around pillars that were once beautiful. Now most of the obsidian and ruby inlaid columns have fallen to the ground, shattered by the crushing strength of the muscled appendages of the tower beast. A faded mural of a stalking panther covers the back wall, the intricate detail in the painting, suggests it was created by a true master of the craft in loving devotion of his vain god. An obsidian altar sits before the masterpiece, an exquisite carving of a goshawk, its wings outstretched once sat upon the volcanic stone, now it has fallen to the floor, one wing snapped off. No doubt the god Cassien has long since abandoned this place, but it still reeks of his power, and the tentacles of the fleshy monster feast off the residual magical energy of the shrine.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Landing]
The nauseating, sweet scent of death fills the air thickly. Coppery scented blood drips from the ceiling in a constant flow, pooling into a depression on the rocky floor. Above, the source of the streaming blood is an immense pulsing blob of shapeless, living flesh. It extends across the entire ceiling, and far into the next rooms. Thick veins of glowing red and blue run through the creature's blubbery flesh, giving off enough light to illuminate the room.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
To the south is what is left of the dormitories of the mages and alchemists that once populated the tower. A soft thumping sound is coming from further down the hallway to the east. The fleshy monster overhead pulses grotesquely, its shapeless body has cemented itself to the stone with hundreds of thousands of hair-like limbs, each ending in an oozing sore. The sap-like fluid seeping from the sores acts like an adhesive, cementing the formless beast to the stone.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Landing]
The nauseating, sweet scent of death fills the air thickly. Coppery scented blood drips from the ceiling in a constant flow, pooling into a depression on the rocky floor. Above, the source of the streaming blood is an immense pulsing blob of shapeless, living flesh. It extends across the entire ceiling, and far into the next rooms. Thick veins of glowing red and blue run through the creature's blubbery flesh, giving off enough light to illuminate the room.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Karishta]
A massive tiled mosaic of a ten-head hydra in the throes of casting some grand, unimaginable spell covers the entire far wall and ceiling of the room. Some tiles have fallen from the image and shattered upon the stony floor, but much of the fantastic masterpiece has survived through the ages. A cracked amethyst altar sits in the center of the room, the worn image of a black rook is carved into the surface of the immense piece of semi-precious stone. Surprisingly, the tower's flesh creature has left much of this room free of its corrupting touch, rather only a few broad tube-like tentacles have attached themselves to the ceiling and have twined around the amethyst altar and disappeared into the base of the deep purple stone.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Devin the Silvarin,  Trion the Fae
In this room are: worn rook-engraved amethyst altar
Obvious exits are: west, north

Devin's group just went north.

You follow Devin...


[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Cassien]
Slender tentacles have crept into the temple of Cassien, unafraid of the tyrant god's wrath, and twined around pillars that were once beautiful. Now most of the obsidian and ruby inlaid columns have fallen to the ground, shattered by the crushing strength of the muscled appendages of the tower beast. A faded mural of a stalking panther covers the back wall, the intricate detail in the painting, suggests it was created by a true master of the craft in loving devotion of his vain god. An obsidian altar sits before the masterpiece, an exquisite carving of a goshawk, its wings outstretched once sat upon the volcanic stone, now it has fallen to the floor, one wing snapped off. No doubt the god Cassien has long since abandoned this place, but it still reeks of his power, and the tentacles of the fleshy monster feast off the residual magical energy of the shrine.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Devin the Silvarin,  Trion the Fae
In this room are: superbly carved obsidian altar
Obvious exits are: south

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Cassien]

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Karishta]
A massive tiled mosaic of a ten-head hydra in the throes of casting some grand, unimaginable spell covers the entire far wall and ceiling of the room. Some tiles have fallen from the image and shattered upon the stony floor, but much of the fantastic masterpiece has survived through the ages. A cracked amethyst altar sits in the center of the room, the worn image of a black rook is carved into the surface of the immense piece of semi-precious stone. Surprisingly, the tower's flesh creature has left much of this room free of its corrupting touch, rather only a few broad tube-like tentacles have attached themselves to the ceiling and have twined around the amethyst altar and disappeared into the base of the deep purple stone.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Shrine to Cassien]
Slender tentacles have crept into the temple of Cassien, unafraid of the tyrant god's wrath, and twined around pillars that were once beautiful. Now most of the obsidian and ruby inlaid columns have fallen to the ground, shattered by the crushing strength of the muscled appendages of the tower beast. A faded mural of a stalking panther covers the back wall, the intricate detail in the painting, suggests it was created by a true master of the craft in loving devotion of his vain god. An obsidian altar sits before the masterpiece, an exquisite carving of a goshawk, its wings outstretched once sat upon the volcanic stone, now it has fallen to the floor, one wing snapped off. No doubt the god Cassien has long since abandoned this place, but it still reeks of his power, and the tentacles of the fleshy monster feast off the residual magical energy of the shrine.

The area is illuminated.

The occasional bird cries out from the rafter perches, and they appear surprisingly well taken care of considering how long the tower has been abandoned, perhaps not all the inhabitants abandoned their birds.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Personal Laboratory]
Charcoal sketches of Zacotl and Goblins are neatly stacked on a wooden table beside a flask magically suspended above a glowing purple flame. The viscous liquid within bubbles violently and the beaker abruptly shatters, sending the volatile fluid spraying in every direction, causing whatever it lands upon to hiss and smoke. A large glinting orb sits in a golden-leafed wrought-iron stand in the center of the room. A jagged crack runs down the center of the sphere, splitting it in two.

In this room you see: 1st roving stray dog
Obvious exits are: south, north, walkway

s

[Southern Cross and Merchants Run]
The cobblestone road intersects with an elegant stone road which heads easterly. A red and black marble arch. The upper portion of the arch frames gold etched lettering that reads "Merchants Walk". Even in the late hours of the evening a traveler to two can be seen making there way east to sell or buy wares.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st urdaian junior guard
Obvious exits are: south, north, arch

s

[Southern Cross Road]
The cobblestone street displays wear in some areas due to the frequent traffic both adventurer and merchant who travel from the city of Travers. A large wooden bulletin with remnants of old parchment paper, makes its home on the western side of the road. Frequently new postings can be found advertising services, festivals and other city happenings.

The area is illuminated.

In this room are: large wooden bulletin board
Obvious exits are: south, north

s

[Southern Cross Road]
Oak trees and Birch trees which house several types of wildlife line the east and west side of the street.  A few small but functional benches are scattered neatly amongst the trees to allow for rest and relaxation. Hanging from a metal pole on the east side of the road is a small black sign with white lettering that reads "This road maintained by the Urda Merchants Council"

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north

s

[Southern Cross Road]
City guards pass by frequently as they make their rounds. To the west the roof of a large building can be seen peaking over a few trees. A small man made waterfall feeds a small pond which bubbles happily on the eastern edge of the road.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Southern Cross Road]
Neatly kept cobblestones lead north and south the cracks between them free of dirt and debris. The large black gate which allows entrance to the city from Travers towers above several trees to the south. To the north the road curves slightly then begins to straighten. The road both north and south is lined on either side by brass lanterns which hang close to the ground from dark walnut posts allowing for plenty of light even on the darkest nights. To the west and the east a neat but narrower road appears to parallel the tall city walls which dots the sky in the distance.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st roving stray dog, 1st urdaian city guard
Obvious exits are: west, south, east, north

w
w

[Popper Street]
Near the main road, a well-dressed Silvarin gentleman sits at the side of Popper Street, nonchalantly reading the local newspaper for the latest issues and rumors. A group of children, diverse in their races, scuffle up to him and beg him for a mark. The Silvarin gives each child a crown and, left to himself and cheers of the children, he reads his paper. The soldiers pass him by quietly, moving on to the Southern Cross Road.

The area is illuminated.

w
Obvious exits are: west, east


[Popper Street]
High-pitched dog growls can be heard from the back of a shady building. The rafters on the roof has fallen in, along with some of the wooden shingles. A young man smiles kindly as the soldiers pass the house by; he enters the house where a young woman can be seen holding a child and attempting to shush the canines. Old bones and scraps are strewn about the front yard beneath a clothes line.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east

w

[Popper Street]
A broken wooden gate swings erratically on its hinges, banging against the old iron lock. Though there is a slight wind, it would not be enough to make the gate swing that hard. Passing the gate, denizens notice three small Zacotl children roughly swinging the gate to make the noise and entertain themselves without parental supervision.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east


[Popper Street]
A shadowy back alley winds its way between the crumbling stone walls of two old storefronts. The abandoned stores have boards over their broken windows and pebbles litter the front porches. One of the roofs have begun to fall into the inside of the store, as wooden beams can be seen dangling down towards the floor through cracks in the wooden boards outside.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st urdaian city guard
Obvious exits are: west, east, dark, narrow alleyway

go al

[Back Alley Bargains]
Planks of wood have been draped over the jagged, crumbling walls of two old buildings, creating a dead end in the alley. A devilishly handsome Duskwen shopkeeper fawns over his "gently used" wares. All manner of clothing, armor, weaponry, and accessories hang from pegs in the store.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: dark, narrow alleyway
